  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\n\ntype Rider = { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string; status: string; vehicle_type: string | null; vehicle_number: string | null }\n\ntype AvailableOrder = {\n  id: string\n  produce_name: string | null\n  quantity: number | null\n  unit: string | null\n  total_price: number | null\n  payment_method: string | null\n  payment_status: string | null\n  delivery_pincode: string | null\n  created_at: string\n  farmer: { name: string; village: string } | null\n}\n\ntype DeliveryStatus = 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery'\n\ntype MyOrder = {\n  id: string\n  produce_name: string | null\n  quantity: number | null\n  unit: string | null\n  total_price: number | null\n  buyer_name: string | null\n  buyer_phone: string | null\n  payment_method: string | null\n  payment_status: string | null\n  delivery_status: DeliveryStatus\n  delivery_address: string | null\n  delivery_landmark: string | null\n  delivery_pincode: string | null\n  delivery_alt_phone: string | null\n  assigned_at: string | null\n  picked_up_at: string | null\n  out_for_delivery_at: string | null\n  farmer: { id: string; name: string; village: string; phone: string | null } | null\n}\n\nexport default function RiderDashboardPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const [rider, setRider] = useState<Rider | null>(null)\n  const [available, setAvailable] = useState<AvailableOrder[]>([])\n  const [mine, setMine] = useState<MyOrder[]>([])\n  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'available' | 'mine'>('available')\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const [otpInputs, setOtpInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})\n  const [otpError, setOtpError] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})\n\n  // Pull current rider once. If the cookie is invalid or the account isn't\n  // active, /api/rider/me returns rider:null and we kick back to login.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false\n    fetch('/api/rider/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' })\n      .then((r) => r.json())\n      .then((json) => {\n        if (cancelled) return\n        if (!json?.rider) { router.replace('/rider/login'); return }\n        setRider(json.rider as Rider)\n      })\n      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) router.replace('/rider/login') })\n    return () => { cancelled = true }\n  }, [router])\n\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    setError('')\n    const r = await fetch('/api/rider/orders', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); setLoading(false); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load orders.'); setLoading(false); return }\n    setAvailable((json.available ?? []) as AvailableOrder[])\n    setMine((json.mine ?? []) as MyOrder[])\n    setLoading(false)\n  }, [])\n\n  // Refresh on mount and every 15 seconds so a rider sees new orders without\n  // having to manually pull. Cheap query — small mandal scale.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!rider) return\n    void refresh()\n    const t = setInterval(() => void refresh(), 15_000)\n    return () => clearInterval(t)\n  }, [rider, refresh])\n\n  const handleLogout = async () => {\n    await fetch('/api/rider/logout', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    router.replace('/rider/login')\n  }\n\n  const callOrderAction = useCallback(async (\n    orderId: string,\n    endpoint: 'accept' | 'pickup' | 'out-for-delivery' | 'deliver',\n    payload?: Record<string, unknown>,\n  ): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> => {\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/rider/orders/${orderId}/${endpoint}`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: payload ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : undefined,\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: payload ? JSON.stringify(payload) : undefined,\n    }).catch(() => null)\n    if (!r) return { ok: false, error: 'Network error.' }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.ok) return { ok: false, error: json?.error ?? 'Action failed.' }\n    return { ok: true }\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleAccept = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const res = await callOrderAction(id, 'accept')\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error); return }\n    setTab('mine')\n    await refresh()\n  }\n\n  const handlePickup = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const res = await callOrderAction(id, 'pickup')\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error); return }\n    await refresh()\n  }\n\n  const handleOutForDelivery = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const res = await callOrderAction(id, 'out-for-delivery')\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error); return }\n    await refresh()\n  }\n\n  const handleDeliver = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    const otp = (otpInputs[id] ?? '').trim()\n    if (!/^\\d{4}$/.test(otp)) {\n      setOtpError((m) => ({ ...m, [id]: 'Enter the 4-digit code from the customer.' }))\n      return\n    }\n    setOtpError((m) => ({ ...m, [id]: '' }))\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const res = await callOrderAction(id, 'deliver', { otp })\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      setOtpError((m) => ({ ...m, [id]: res.error }))\n      return\n    }\n    setOtpInputs((m) => ({ ...m, [id]: '' }))\n    await refresh()\n  }\n\n  if (!rider) {\n    return (\n      <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </main>\n    )\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16\">\n      <div className=\"bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-3\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <p className=\"text-green-300 text-xs\">Delivery partner / డెలివరీ పార్ట్నర్</p>\n            <h1 className=\"text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight truncate\">{rider.name || rider.phone}</h1>\n            <p className=\"text-green-400 text-xs mt-0.5\">\n              {rider.vehicle_type ? `${rider.vehicle_type} · ` : ''}{rider.vehicle_number ?? ''}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n          <button\n            onClick={handleLogout}\n            className=\"text-green-200 text-xs underline whitespace-nowrap\"\n          >\n            Log out\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"px-4 -mt-6 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto\">\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-1.5 flex gap-1.5 shadow-sm\">\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setTab('available')}\n            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${\n              tab === 'available' ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-white text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-50'\n            }`}\n          >\n            Available ({available.length})\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setTab('mine')}\n            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${\n              tab === 'mine' ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-white text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-50'\n            }`}\n          >\n            My deliveries ({mine.length})\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {error && (\n          <div className=\"bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 font-semibold\">{error}</p>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {loading ? (\n          <div className=\"text-center py-10\">\n            <div className=\"w-8 h-8 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : tab === 'available' ? (\n          available.length === 0 ? (\n            <div className=\"text-center py-14 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100\">\n              <div className=\"text-5xl mb-3\">📭</div>\n              <p className=\"font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm\">No orders waiting right now</p>\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1\">ఇప్పుడు ఏ ఆర్డర్‌లు లేవు</p>\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              {available.map((o) => (\n                <AvailableCard\n                  key={o.id}\n                  order={o}\n                  onAccept={() => handleAccept(o.id)}\n                  busy={busyId === o.id}\n                />\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )\n        ) : mine.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"text-center py-14 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100\">\n            <div className=\"text-5xl mb-3\">🛵</div>\n            <p className=\"font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm\">No active deliveries</p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1\">Accept an order from the Available tab</p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {mine.map((o) => (\n              <MyOrderCard\n                key={o.id}\n                order={o}\n                busy={busyId === o.id}\n                otp={otpInputs[o.id] ?? ''}\n                onOtpChange={(v) => setOtpInputs((m) => ({ ...m, [o.id]: v }))}\n                otpError={otpError[o.id]}\n                onPickup={() => handlePickup(o.id)}\n                onOutForDelivery={() => handleOutForDelivery(o.id)}\n                onDeliver={() => handleDeliver(o.id)}\n              />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction AvailableCard({\n  order,\n  onAccept,\n  busy,\n}: {\n  order: AvailableOrder\n  onAccept: () => void\n  busy: boolean\n}) {\n  const isCod = order.payment_method === 'cod'\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden\">\n      <div className=\"p-4 space-y-2\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <p className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight\">\n              {order.produce_name || 'Order'}\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n              {order.quantity ?? 0} {order.unit || 'kg'}\n              {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n          {isCod ? (\n            <span className=\"text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 whitespace-nowrap\">\n              Collect ₹{order.total_price ?? 0} cash\n            </span>\n          ) : (\n            <span className=\"text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800 whitespace-nowrap\">\n              Already paid\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-2 space-y-1.5\">\n          <p className=\"text-xs\">\n            <span className=\"font-bold text-green-800\">🧑‍🌾 Pickup:</span>{' '}\n            {order.farmer ? `${order.farmer.name} · ${order.farmer.village}` : 'Farmer'}\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-xs\">\n            <span className=\"font-bold text-blue-800\">📍 Drop area:</span>{' '}\n            {order.delivery_pincode || 'Within mandal'}\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 leading-snug\">\n            Full address &amp; phone numbers shown after you accept.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <button\n          onClick={onAccept}\n          disabled={busy}\n          className=\"w-full mt-1 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {busy ? 'Accepting...' : '✓ Accept this delivery'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction MyOrderCard({\n  order,\n  busy,\n  otp,\n  onOtpChange,\n  otpError,\n  onPickup,\n  onOutForDelivery,\n  onDeliver,\n}: {\n  order: MyOrder\n  busy: boolean\n  otp: string\n  onOtpChange: (v: string) => void\n  otpError?: string\n  onPickup: () => void\n  onOutForDelivery: () => void\n  onDeliver: () => void\n}) {\n  const isCod = order.payment_method === 'cod'\n  const statusLabel =\n    order.delivery_status === 'assigned' ? 'Go pick up'\n      : order.delivery_status === 'picked_up' ? 'Picked up'\n      : 'Out for delivery'\n  const statusColor =\n    order.delivery_status === 'assigned' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'\n      : order.delivery_status === 'picked_up' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800'\n      : 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800'\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden\">\n      <div className=\"p-4 space-y-3\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <p className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight\">\n              {order.produce_name || 'Order'}\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n              {order.quantity ?? 0} {order.unit || 'kg'}\n              {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${statusColor} whitespace-nowrap`}>\n            {statusLabel}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {isCod && (\n          <div className=\"bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs\">\n            <span className=\"font-bold text-amber-900\">💵 Collect ₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span> in cash from the customer.\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Pickup details */}\n        {order.farmer && (\n          <div className=\"border border-green-100 bg-green-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 space-y-1\">\n            <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-green-800 uppercase tracking-wide\">Pickup from / పికప్</p>\n            <p className=\"text-sm font-bold text-gray-900\">{order.farmer.name}</p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600\">{order.farmer.village}</p>\n            {order.farmer.phone && (\n              <a\n                href={`tel:${order.farmer.phone}`}\n                className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 text-xs font-bold text-green-700 underline\"\n              >\n                📞 Call farmer · {order.farmer.phone}\n              </a>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Drop details */}\n        <div className=\"border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 space-y-1\">\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-800 uppercase tracking-wide\">Deliver to / డెలివరీ</p>\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-bold text-gray-900\">{order.buyer_name || 'Customer'}</p>\n          {order.delivery_address && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-700 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line\">{order.delivery_address}</p>\n          )}\n          {order.delivery_landmark && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600\">📍 {order.delivery_landmark}</p>\n          )}\n          {order.delivery_pincode && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600\">PIN: {order.delivery_pincode}</p>\n          )}\n          {order.buyer_phone && (\n            <a\n              href={`tel:${order.buyer_phone}`}\n              className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 text-xs font-bold text-blue-700 underline\"\n            >\n              📞 Call customer · {order.buyer_phone}\n            </a>\n          )}\n          {order.delivery_alt_phone && order.delivery_alt_phone !== order.buyer_phone && (\n            <a\n              href={`tel:${order.delivery_alt_phone}`}\n              className=\"block text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 underline\"\n            >\n              Alt: {order.delivery_alt_phone}\n            </a>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Action */}\n        {order.delivery_status === 'assigned' && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onPickup}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {busy ? 'Updating...' : '📦 Mark as picked up'}\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {order.delivery_status === 'picked_up' && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onOutForDelivery}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"w-full bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {busy ? 'Updating...' : '🛵 Out for delivery'}\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {order.delivery_status === 'out_for_delivery' && (\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <label className=\"text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block\">\n              4-digit code from customer / కస్టమర్ నుండి కోడ్\n            </label>\n            <input\n              type=\"tel\"\n              inputMode=\"numeric\"\n              maxLength={4}\n              value={otp}\n              onChange={(e) => onOtpChange(e.target.value.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}\n              placeholder=\"0000\"\n              className=\"w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-2xl font-mono font-bold tracking-widest text-center focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n            />\n            {otpError && (\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 font-semibold\">{otpError}</p>\n            )}\n            <button\n              onClick={onDeliver}\n              disabled={busy}\n              className=\"w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n            >\n              {busy ? 'Confirming...' : '✓ Confirm delivered'}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { randomInt } from 'crypto'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'\nimport { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\ntype IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }\n\n// 4-digit handover code, generated server-side at order placement. Customer\n// reads it off their order page and reads it aloud to the rider at the door.\n// crypto.randomInt avoids Math.random's predictability.\nfunction generateHandoverOtp(): string {\n  return String(randomInt(0, 10000)).padStart(4, '0')\n}"
}

```
1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
4	import { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'
5	
6	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
7	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
8	
9	type IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }
10	
11	type ListingRow = {
12	  id: string
13	  name: string
14	  unit: string | null
15	  stock_qty: number | null
16	  farmer_id: string
17	  price_tier_1_qty: number | null
18	  price_tier_1_price: number | null
19	  price_tier_2_qty: number | null
20	  price_tier_2_price: number | null
21	  price_tier_3_price: number | null
22	}
23	
24	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
25	
26	function bad(msg: string, status = 400) {
27	  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })
28	}
29	
30	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
31	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
32	  if (!session) return bad('Please log in to place an order.', 401)
33	
34	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null) as
35	    | {
36	        farmerId?: string
37	        paymentMethod?: string
38	        pickupLocation?: string | null
39	        pickupDay?: string | null
40	        items?: IncomingItem[]
41	      }
42	    | null
43	
44	  if (!body) return bad('Invalid request body.')
45	  // pickupDay is currently UI-only (not a DB column); accept and ignore.
46	  const { farmerId, paymentMethod, pickupLocation, items } = body
47	
48	  if (!farmerId || !UUID_RE.test(farmerId)) return bad('Invalid farmer.')
49	  if (paymentMethod !== 'upi' && paymentMethod !== 'cod') return bad('Invalid payment method.')
50	  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return bad('Cart is empty.')
51	  if (items.length > 50) return bad('Too many items.')
52	  for (const it of items) {
53	    if (!it || typeof it !== 'object') return bad('Invalid item.')
54	    if (!it.listingId || !UUID_RE.test(it.listingId)) return bad('Invalid listing id.')
55	    if (!Number.isFinite(it.qty) || it.qty <= 0 || it.qty > 10000) return bad('Invalid quantity.')
56	  }
57	
58	  const supabase = createClient(
59	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
60	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
61	  )
62	
63	  // Authoritative consumer profile (we never trust the client for buyer name/phone)
64	  const { data: consumer } = await supabase
65	    .from('consumers_auth')
66	    .select('id, name, phone')
67	    .eq('id', session.consumerId)
68	    .maybeSingle()
69	
70	  if (!consumer) return bad('Account not found. Please log in again.', 401)
71	
72	  // Farmer COD acceptance check
73	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
74	    .from('farmers')
75	    .select('id, cod_enabled')
76	    .eq('id', farmerId)
77	    .maybeSingle()
78	
79	  if (!farmer) return bad('Farmer not found.', 404)
80	  if (paymentMethod === 'cod' && farmer.cod_enabled !== true) {
81	    return bad('This farmer is not accepting Cash on Delivery.')
82	  }
83	
84	  // Pull live listing rows — never trust prices from the client cart
85	  const listingIds = items.map((i) => i.listingId)
86	  const { data: listings } = await supabase
87	    .from('produce_listings')
88	    .select(
89	      'id, name, unit, stock_qty, farmer_id, price_tier_1_qty, price_tier_1_price, price_tier_2_qty, price_tier_2_price, price_tier_3_price',
90	    )
91	    .in('id', listingIds) as { data: ListingRow[] | null }
92	
93	  if (!listings || listings.length !== listingIds.length) {
94	    return bad('One or more items in your cart are no longer available.')
95	  }
96	
97	  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))
98	  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []
99	  let total = 0
100	
101	  for (const item of items) {
102	    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)
103	    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')
104	    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')
105	    if (listing.stock_qty != null && item.qty > listing.stock_qty) {
106	      return bad(`Only ${listing.stock_qty} ${listing.unit || 'kg'} of ${listing.name} available right now.`)
107	    }
108	
109	    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {
110	      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,
111	      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,
112	      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,
113	      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,
114	      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,
115	    })
116	
117	    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null
118	    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {
119	      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)
120	    }
121	    total += linePrice
122	
123	    rows.push({
124	      farmer_id: farmerId,
125	      produce_listing_id: listing.id,
126	      produce_name: listing.name,
127	      quantity: item.qty,
128	      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',
129	      total_price: linePrice,
130	      buyer_name: consumer.name || 'Buyer',
131	      buyer_phone: consumer.phone,
132	      consumer_id: consumer.id,
133	      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,
134	      status: 'pending',
135	      payment_method: paymentMethod,
136	      payment_status: 'pending',
137	    })
138	  }
139	
140	  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase
141	    .from('orders')
142	    .insert(rows)
143	    .select('id')
144	
145	  if (insertErr || !inserted) {
146	    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)
147	    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)
148	  }
149	
150	  return NextResponse.json({
151	    ok: true,
152	    orderIds: inserted.map((r) => r.id),
153	    total,
154	  })
155	}
156	
```

  "new_string": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { randomInt } from 'crypto'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'\nimport { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\ntype IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }\n\n// 4-digit handover code, generated server-side at order placement. The\n// customer reads it off their order page and reads it aloud to the rider at\n// the door. crypto.randomInt avoids Math.random's predictability.\nfunction generateHandoverOtp(): string {\n  return String(randomInt(0, 10000)).padStart(4, '0')\n}"
}

```
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i

export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Login required.' }, { status: 401 })

  const { id } = await ctx.params
  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  const { data: order, error } = await supabase
    .from('orders')
    .select(
      'id, consumer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_status, decline_reason, payment_proof_path, created_at, farmer_id',
    )
    .eq('id', id)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
  }

  // Manual farmer join (no FK on orders.farmer_id → farmers.id, so embedding fails)
  let farmer: { name: string; slug: string; village: string; phone: string | null; upi_id: string | null } | null = null
  if (order.farmer_id) {
    const { data: f } = await supabase
      .from('farmers')
      .select('id, name, slug, village, phone, upi_id')
      .eq('id', order.farmer_id)
      .maybeSingle()
    if (f) {
      farmer = {
        name: f.name,
        slug: f.slug,
        village: f.village,
        phone: f.phone ?? null,
        upi_id: (f.upi_id as string | null) ?? null,
      }
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ order: { ...order, farmer } })
}
---

  "new_string": "  // Pull rider contact for any order that's been assigned, so the consumer\n  // can call the delivery boy at their door. We only ever expose name + phone\n  // — never the rider's address, ID proof, or password fields.\n  const riderIds = [...new Set((orders ?? []).map((o) => (o as { delivery_boy_id?: string | null }).delivery_boy_id).filter((v): v is string => !!v))]\n  let riderMap: Record<string, { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string }> = {}\n  if (riderIds.length > 0) {\n    const { data: riders } = await supabase\n      .from('delivery_boys')\n      .select('id, name, phone')\n      .in('id', riderIds)\n    riderMap = Object.fromEntries(\n      (riders ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, { id: r.id, name: r.name ?? null, phone: r.phone }]),\n    )\n  }\n\n  const enriched = (orders ?? []).map((o) => {\n    const row = o as Record<string, unknown>\n    const riderId = (row.delivery_boy_id as string | null) ?? null\n    return {\n      ...o,\n      farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,\n      rider: riderId ? riderMap[riderId] ?? null : null,\n    }\n  })\n  return NextResponse.json({ orders: enriched })\n}"
}

8	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
9	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
10	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ orders: [] }, { status: 200 })
11

28	  }

31	  // embed the farmer resource for us. One extra query keeps it simple.
32	  const farmerIds = [...new Set((orders ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]
33	  let farmerMap: Record<string, { name: string; slug: string; village: string; phone: string | null; upi_id: string | null }> = {}

  "new_string": "          </>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction DeliveryPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {\n  const ds: DeliveryStatus = (order.delivery_status as DeliveryStatus) || 'unassigned'\n\n  const steps: Array<{ key: DeliveryStatus; label: string; sub: string; at: string | null | undefined }> = [\n    { key: 'unassigned', label: 'Order placed', sub: 'Waiting for a delivery boy', at: order.created_at },\n    { key: 'assigned', label: 'Rider assigned', sub: 'On the way to farmer', at: order.assigned_at },\n    { key: 'picked_up', label: 'Picked up', sub: 'Collected from farmer', at: order.picked_up_at },\n    { key: 'out_for_delivery', label: 'Out for delivery', sub: 'On the way to you', at: order.out_for_delivery_at },\n    { key: 'delivered', label: 'Delivered', sub: 'Order completed', at: order.delivered_at },\n  ]\n\n  const stageIndex = (k: DeliveryStatus) => ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'picked_up', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered'].indexOf(k)\n  const current = stageIndex(ds)\n\n  const formatAt = (iso: string | null | undefined) => {\n    if (!iso) return ''\n    const d = new Date(iso)\n    return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n        <span className=\"text-base\">🛵</span>\n        <p className=\"text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide\">\n          Home delivery / ఇంటికి డెలివరీ\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Handover OTP — visible once a rider has been assigned. Customer\n          reads this 4-digit code to the rider at the door so the system\n          can confirm the right person received the goods. */}\n      {ds !== 'unassigned' && ds !== 'delivered' && order.handover_otp && (\n        <div className=\"bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide\">\n            Read this code to the delivery boy at your door\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] text-amber-700 mt-0.5\">\n            డోర్ వద్ద డెలివరీ బాయ్‌కు ఈ కోడ్ చెప్పండి\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono\">\n            {order.handover_otp}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {ds === 'delivered' && (\n        <div className=\"bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800\">✓ Delivered</p>\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5\">{formatAt(order.delivered_at)}</p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Status timeline */}\n      <ol className=\"space-y-2\">\n        {steps.map((s, idx) => {\n          const reached = idx <= current\n          const isCurrent = idx === current\n          return (\n            <li key={s.key} className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5\">\n                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />\n                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (\n                  <span className={`w-0.5 flex-1 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />\n                )}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex-1 pb-1\">\n                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>\n                <p className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug\">{s.sub}</p>\n                {s.at && reached && (\n                  <p className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{formatAt(s.at)}</p>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </li>\n          )\n        })}\n      </ol>\n\n      {/* Delivery address (always visible — confirms what consumer entered) */}\n      {order.delivery_address && (\n        <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-3\">\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1\">Delivering to</p>\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-800 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line\">{order.delivery_address}</p>\n          {order.delivery_landmark && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600 mt-0.5\">📍 {order.delivery_landmark}</p>\n          )}\n          {order.delivery_pincode && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600\">PIN: {order.delivery_pincode}</p>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Rider contact — only when assigned (and not yet delivered) */}\n      {order.rider && ds !== 'delivered' && (\n        <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-3\">\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide\">Delivery boy / డెలివరీ బాయ్</p>\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mt-1\">{order.rider.name || 'Your delivery partner'}</p>\n          <a\n            href={`tel:${order.rider.phone}`}\n            className=\"mt-2 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-blue-700\"\n          >\n            📞 Call · {order.rider.phone}\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}"
}

'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
import LocationSearch from '@/components/LocationSearch'
import { FreshnessBadge } from '@/components/FreshnessBadge'

type PickupSlots = {
  days: string[]
  time_from: string
  time_to: string
}

type Farmer = {
  id: string
  name: string
  slug: string
  village: string
  district: string
  phone: string
  method: string
  region_slug: string
  rating_avg: number | null
  buyer_count: number
  farming_since_year: number | null
  pickup_locations: string[] | null
  cover_photo_url: string | null
  photo_url: string | null
  pesticide_cert_url: string | null
  pickup_slots: PickupSlots | null
  lat: number | null
  lng: number | null
  location_name: string | null
  upi_id: string | null
  upi_qr_code_url: string | null
  cod_enabled: boolean | null
}

type DemandBar = {
  crop_name: string
  total_qty: number
}

type ListingRow = {
  id: string
  name: string
  variety: string | null
  emoji: string | null
  status: string
  method: string | null
  stock_qty: number | null
  price_tier_1_price: number | null
  price_tier_1_qty: number | null
  price_tier_2_price: number | null
  price_tier_2_qty: number | null
  price_tier_3_price: number | null
  description: string | null
  image_url: string | null
  brix: number | null
  soil_organic_carbon: number | null
  unit: string | null
  harvest_date: string | null
  created_at: string
}

type Order = {
  id: string
  farmer_id: string
  produce_listing_id: string | null
  produce_name: string | null
  quantity: number | null
  unit: string | null
  total_price: number | null
  buyer_name: string | null
  buyer_phone: string | null
  pickup_location: string | null
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
  payment_method: string | null
  payment_status: string | null
  utr_number: string | null
  decline_reason: string | null
  created_at: string
}

const UNIT_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'kg', label: 'kg' },
  { value: 'gram', label: 'gram' },
  { value: 'piece', label: 'piece / నగ' },
  { value: 'bunch', label: 'bunch / కట్ట' },
  { value: 'litre', label: 'litre' },
]

type PreviewData = {
  name: string
  variety: string
  emoji: string
  price: string
  method: string
  stock: string
}

const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾', '🥥']

async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const img = new Image()
    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)

function FarmerNotFound({ onLogout }: { onLogout: () => void }) {
  const { tx } = useLang()
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4">
      <div className="text-center max-w-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="text-6xl">🌾</div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">{tx.sessionExpired}</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.sessionExpiredHelp}</p>
        <button onClick={onLogout} className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm">
          {tx.loginAgain}
        </button>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

(\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 truncate\">{rider.name || 'Rider'}</p>\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500\">For pickup coordination</p>\n          </div>\n          <a\n            href={`tel:${rider.phone}`}\n            className=\"bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-xl whitespace-nowrap active:bg-blue-700\"\n          >\n            📞 Call · {rider.phone}\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <p className=\"text-[11px] text-blue-700\">\n          A delivery boy will pick up the order. {
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/login/route.ts",
  "content": "import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getAdminPassword, setAdminSessionCookie } from '@/lib/admin-session'\nimport { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\nimport { timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nfunction safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {\n  const A = Buffer.from(a)\n  const B = Buffer.from(b)\n  if (A.length !== B.length) return false\n  return timingSafeEqual(A, B)\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'\n  if (!rateLimit(`admin-login:${ip}`, 5, 15 * 60 * 1000)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many attempts. Wait 15 min.' }, { status: 429 })\n  }\n\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)\n  const password = String((body && (body as { password?: unknown }).password) ?? '')\n  if (!password) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password required.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  let expected: string\n  try {\n    expected = getAdminPassword()\n  } catch (e) {\n    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Server misconfigured.'\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  if (!safeEqual(password, expected)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Wrong password.' }, { status: 401 })\n  }\n\n  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n  setAdminSessionCookie(res)\n  return res\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/riders/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: riders, error } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, name, phone, alt_phone, vehicle_type, vehicle_number, service_areas, status, activation_code, id_proof_path, approved_at, activated_at, last_login_at, created_at')\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    .limit(200)\n\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/riders] query failed:', error.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  // Sign ID-proof URLs in batch so the panel can show the photo without\n  // exposing the bucket. URLs expire in 10 min — the admin should refresh.\n  const withProofs = await Promise.all(\n    (riders ?? []).map(async (r) => {\n      let idProofUrl: string | null = null\n      if (r.id_proof_path) {\n        const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage\n          .from('rider-id-proofs')\n          .createSignedUrl(r.id_proof_path, 600)\n        idProofUrl = signed?.signedUrl ?? null\n      }\n      return { ...r, id_proof_url: idProofUrl }\n    }),\n  )\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ riders: withProofs })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/riders/[id]/approve/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { randomBytes } from 'crypto'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\n// Friendly alphabet: no 0/O, no 1/I/L — easy to read aloud over the phone.\nconst CODE_ALPHABET = 'REDACTED'\n\nfunction generateActivationCode(): string {\n  const bytes = randomBytes(8)\n  let out = ''\n  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) out += CODE_ALPHABET[bytes[i] % CODE_ALPHABET.length]\n  return `YFF-${out}`\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await params\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status, activation_code')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (rider.status === 'active') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is already active.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n  if (rider.status === 'suspended') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is suspended. Reinstate first.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n\n  // If a prior approval issued a code that hasn't been consumed, reuse it\n  // so the admin doesn't have to call the rider again with a new one.\n  const code = rider.activation_code || generateActivationCode()\n\n  const { error: updErr } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .update({\n      status: 'approved',\n      activation_code: code,\n      approved_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    })\n    .eq('id', id)\n\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/approve] update failed:', updErr.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: updErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, activation_code: code })\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/riders/[id]/reinstate/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await params\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // If they were previously activated, go straight back to active; otherwise\n  // drop them back to pending_approval so the admin can re-approve.\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, activated_at')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n\n  const nextStatus = rider.activated_at ? 'active' : 'pending_approval'\n  const { error } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .update({ status: nextStatus })\n    .eq('id', id)\n\n  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, status: nextStatus })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/deliveries/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Every home-delivery order, newest first. The admin is the safety net for\n  // anything stuck (e.g. no rider accepted) so we include unassigned rows too.\n  const { data: orders, error } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select(\n      'id, farmer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, buyer_name, buyer_phone, status, payment_method, payment_status, delivery_type, delivery_status, delivery_address, delivery_landmark, delivery_pincode, delivery_alt_phone, delivery_boy_id, handover_otp, assigned_at, picked_up_at, out_for_delivery_at, delivered_at, created_at',\n    )\n    .eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    .limit(200)\n\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/deliveries] query failed:', error.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  const farmerIds = [...new Set((orders ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]\n  const riderIds = [...new Set((orders ?? []).map((o) => o.delivery_boy_id).filter((v): v is string => !!v))]\n\n  let farmerMap: Record<string, { id: string; name: string; village: string; phone: string | null }> = {}\n  if (farmerIds.length > 0) {\n    const { data: farmers } = await supabase\n      .from('farmers')\n      .select('id, name, village, phone')\n      .in('id', farmerIds)\n    farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(\n      (farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, { id: f.id, name: f.name, village: f.village, phone: f.phone ?? null }]),\n    )\n  }\n\n  let riderMap: Record<string, { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string }> = {}\n  if (riderIds.length > 0) {\n    const { data: riders } = await supabase\n      .from('delivery_boys')\n      .select('id, name, phone')\n      .in('id', riderIds)\n    riderMap = Object.fromEntries(\n      (riders ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, { id: r.id, name: r.name ?? null, phone: r.phone }]),\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Also expose the list of currently active riders so the admin can reassign\n  // straight from the same payload — saves a second round-trip.\n  const { data: activeRiders } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, name, phone, vehicle_number')\n    .eq('status', 'active')\n    .order('name', { ascending: true })\n\n  const enriched = (orders ?? []).map((o) => ({\n    ...o,\n    farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,\n    rider: o.delivery_boy_id ? riderMap[o.delivery_boy_id] ?? null : null,\n  }))\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ orders: enriched, riders: activeRiders ?? [] })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/admin/orders/[id]/reassign/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await params\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)\n  const riderId = String((body && (body as { riderId?: unknown }).riderId) ?? '')\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Confirm the order is a home delivery that's still in flight. We won't\n  // reassign a delivered order.\n  const { data: order } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select('id, delivery_type, delivery_status, status')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (order.delivery_type !== 'home_delivery') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not a delivery order.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n  if (order.delivery_status === 'delivered') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order is already delivered.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n\n  // Special case: empty riderId = un-assign. Useful when the assigned rider\n  // is unreachable and the admin wants to release the order back to the\n  // available queue.\n  if (!riderId) {\n    const { error } = await supabase\n      .from('orders')\n      .update({\n        delivery_boy_id: null,\n        delivery_status: 'unassigned',\n        assigned_at: null,\n        picked_up_at: null,\n        out_for_delivery_at: null,\n      })\n      .eq('id', id)\n    if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n  }\n\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(riderId)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status')\n    .eq('id', riderId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (rider.status !== 'active') return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is not active.' }, { status: 409 })\n\n  const { error } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .update({\n      delivery_boy_id: riderId,\n      delivery_status: 'assigned',\n      assigned_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n      // Wipe later-stage timestamps if reassigning from a later state — the\n      // new rider starts from scratch.\n      picked_up_at: null,\n      out_for_delivery_at: null,\n    })\n    .eq('id', id)\n\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/reassign] update failed:', error.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n}\n"
}

  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\n\nexport default function AdminLoginPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)\n\n  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    if (submitting) return\n    setError('')\n    setSubmitting(true)\n    const r = await fetch('/api/admin/login', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ password }),\n    }).catch(() => null)\n    setSubmitting(false)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Login failed.'); return }\n    router.replace('/admin')\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col\">\n      <div className=\"bg-gray-900 px-4 pt-10 pb-14\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-white text-2xl font-extrabold\">Owner panel</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1\">Internal · password protected</p>\n      </div>\n      <form\n        onSubmit={submit}\n        className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-md mx-4 -mt-6 p-5 space-y-4 max-w-md w-full mx-auto\"\n      >\n        <div>\n          <label className=\"text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">\n            Admin password\n          </label>\n          <input\n            type=\"password\"\n            value={password}\n            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}\n            className=\"w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n            required\n            autoFocus\n          />\n        </div>\n        {error && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 font-semibold\">\n            {error}\n          </p>\n        )}\n        <button\n          type=\"submit\"\n          disabled={submitting}\n          className=\"w-full bg-gray-900 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base active:bg-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {submitting ? 'Checking...' : 'Log in'}\n        </button>\n      </form>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n"
}

  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\n\ntype Rider = {\n  id: string\n  name: string | null\n  phone: string\n  alt_phone: string | null\n  vehicle_type: string | null\n  vehicle_number: string | null\n  service_areas: string | null\n  status: 'pending_approval' | 'approved' | 'active' | 'suspended'\n  activation_code: string | null\n  id_proof_url: string | null\n  approved_at: string | null\n  activated_at: string | null\n  last_login_at: string | null\n  created_at: string\n}\n\ntype DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'\n\ntype DeliveryOrder = {\n  id: string\n  produce_name: string | null\n  quantity: number | null\n  unit: string | null\n  total_price: number | null\n  buyer_name: string | null\n  buyer_phone: string | null\n  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'\n  payment_method: string | null\n  payment_status: string | null\n  delivery_status: DeliveryStatus | null\n  delivery_address: string | null\n  delivery_landmark: string | null\n  delivery_pincode: string | null\n  delivery_alt_phone: string | null\n  delivery_boy_id: string | null\n  handover_otp: string | null\n  assigned_at: string | null\n  picked_up_at: string | null\n  out_for_delivery_at: string | null\n  delivered_at: string | null\n  created_at: string\n  farmer: { id: string; name: string; village: string; phone: string | null } | null\n  rider: { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string } | null\n}\n\ntype ActiveRider = { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string; return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load riders.'); return }\n    setRiders((json.riders ?? []) as Rider[])\n  }, [])\n\n  const loadDeliveries = useCallback(async () => {\n    const r = await fetch('/api/admin/deliveries', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load deliveries.'); return }\n    setOrders((json.orders ?? []) as DeliveryOrder[])\n    setActiveRiders((json.riders ?? []) as ActiveRider[])\n  }, [])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!authed) return\n    void loadRiders()\n    void loadDeliveries()\n    const t = setInterval(() => {\n      void loadRiders()\n      void loadDeliveries()\n    }, 20_000)\n    return () => clearInterval(t)\n  }, [authed, loadRiders, loadDeliveries])\n\n  const handleApprove = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/admin/riders/${id}/approve`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Approve failed.'); return }\n    if (json?.activation_code) {\n      setApprovalCodes((m) => ({ ...m, [id]: json.activation_code }))\n    }\n    await loadRiders()\n  }\n\n  const handleSuspend = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    if (!confirm('Suspend this rider? They will not be able to log in.')) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/admin/riders/${id}/suspend`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Suspend failed.'); return }\n    await loadRiders()\n  }\n\n  const handleReinstate = async (id: string) => {\n    if (busyId) return\n    setBusyId(id)\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/admin/riders/${id}/reinstate`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    setBusyId(null)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Reinstate failed.'); return }\n    await loadRiders()\n  }\n\n  const handleReassign = async (orderId: string) => {\n    const targetRider = reassignTarget[orderId] ?? 'Reassign failed.'); return }\n    setReassignTarget((m) => ({ ...m, [orderId]: '' }))\n    await loadDeliveries()\n  }\n\n  const handleLogout = async () => {\n    await fetch('/api/admin/logout', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    router.replace('/admin/login')\n  }\n\n  if (authed === null) {\n    return (\n      <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </main>\n    )\n  }\n  if (!authed) return null\n\n  const pendingRiders = riders.filter((r) => r.status === 'pending_approval' || r.status === 'approved')\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16\">\n      <div className=\"bg-gray-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-3\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-xs\">Owner panel</p>\n            <h1 className=\"text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight\">YFF Deliveries</h1>\n          </div>\n          <button onClick={handleLogout} className=\"text-gray-300 text-xs underline whitespace-nowrap\">\n            Log out\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"px-4 -mt-6 space-y-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto\">\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-1.5 flex gap-1.5 shadow-sm\">\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setTab('deliveries')}\n            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${\n              tab === 'deliveries' ? 'bg-gray-900 text-white' : 'bg-white text-gray-600'\n            }`}\n          >\n            Riders ({riders.length}{pendingRiders.length > 0 ? ` · ${pendingRiders.length} pending` : ''})\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {error && (\n          <div className=\"bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 font-semibold\">{error}</p>\n            <button onClick={() => setError('')} className=\"text-[10px] text-red-600 underline mt-1\">Dismiss</button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {tab === 'deliveries' ? (\n          orders.length === 0 ? (\n            <div className=\"text-center py-14 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100\">\n              <div className=\"text-5xl mb-3\">📭</div>\n              <p className=\"font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm\">No delivery orders yet</p>\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              {orders.map((o) => (\n                <DeliveryRow\n                  key={o.id}\n                  order={o}\n                  activeRiders={activeRiders}\n                  target={reassignTarget[o.id] ?? ''}\n                  onTargetChange={(v) => setReassignTarget((m) => ({ ...m, [o.id]: v }))}\n                  onReassign={() => handleReassign(o.id)}\n                  busy={busyId === o.id}\n                />\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )\n        ) : riders.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"text-center py-14 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100\">\n            <div className=\"text-5xl mb-3\">🛵</div>\n            <p className=\"font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm\">No rider applications yet</p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {riders.map((r) => (\n              <RiderRow\n                key={r.id}\n                rider={r}\n                code={approvalCodes[r.id]}\n                onApprove={() => handleApprove(r.id)}\n                onSuspend={() => handleSuspend(r.id)}\n                onReinstate={() => handleReinstate(r.id)}\n                busy={busyId === r.id}\n              />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction DeliveryRow({\n  order,\n  activeRiders,\n  target,\n  onTargetChange,\n  onReassign,\n  busy,\n}: {\n  order: DeliveryOrder\n  activeRiders: ActiveRider[]\n  target: string\n  onTargetChange: (v: string) => void\n  onReassign: () => void\n  busy: boolean\n}) {\n  const ds = order.delivery_status ?? 'unassigned'\n  const statusColor =\n    ds === 'delivered' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'\n      : ds === 'out_for_delivery' ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800'\n      : ds === 'picked_up' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'\n      : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-2\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n        <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n          <p className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight\">\n            {order.produce_name || 'Order'} · {order.quantity ?? 0} {order.unit ?? '...' : target ? 'Reassign' : 'Un-assign'}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction RiderRow({\n  rider,\n  code,\n  onApprove,\n  onSuspend,\n  onReinstate,\n  busy,\n}: {\n  rider: Rider\n  code?: string\n  onApprove: () => void\n  onSuspend: () => void\n  onReinstate: () => void\n  busy: boolean\n}) {\n  const statusColor =\n    rider.status === 'active' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800'\n      : 'bg-red-100 text-red-800'\n\n  const liveCode = code || rider.activation_code\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 leading-tight\">{rider.name || '—'}</p>\n          <a href={`tel:${rider.phone}`} className=\"text-xs text-blue-700 underline\">📞 {rider.phone}</a>\n          {rider.alt_phone && rider.alt_phone !== rider.phone && (\n            <a href={`tel:${rider.alt_phone}`} className=\"block text-xs text-blue-700 underline\">Alt: {rider.alt_phone}</a>\n          )}\n        </div>\n        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor}`}>\n          {rider.status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"text-xs grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n        <div>\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase\">Vehicle</p>\n          <p className=\"text-gray-800\">{rider.vehicle_type || '—'} · {rider.vehicle_number || '—'}</p>\n        </div>\n        <div>\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase\">Service areas</p>\n          <p className=\"text-gray-800 whitespace-pre-line\">{rider.service_areas || '—'}</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {rider.id_proof_url && (\n        <div>\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1\">ID proof</p>\n          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n          <img\n )}\n\n      {(rider.status === 'approved') && liveCode && (\n        <div className=\"bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-[10px] {rider.status === 'pending_approval' && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onApprove}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"flex-1 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {busy ? '...' : 'Approve & generate code'}\n          </button>\n        )}\n        {rider.status === 'approved' && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onApprove}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"flex-1 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs active:bg-amber-700 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {busy ? '...' : 'Show / re-issue code'}\n          </button>\n        )}\n        {(rider.status === 'active' || rider.status === 'approved') && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onSuspend}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"flex-1 border border-red-300 text-red-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs active:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            Suspend\n          </button>\n        )}\n        {rider.status === 'suspended' && (\n          <button\n            onClick={onReinstate}\n            disabled={busy}\n            className=\"flex-1 bg-gray-900 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs active:bg-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {busy ? '...' : 'Reinstate'}\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-3 text-[10px] text-gray-400 border-t border-gray-100 pt-2\">\n        <div>\n          <p>Applied</p>\n          <p>{new Date(rider.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN')}</p>\n        </div>\n        <div>\n          <p>Activated</p>\n          <p>{rider.activated_at ? new Date(rider.activated_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN') : '—'}</p>\n        </div>\n        <div>\n          <p>Last login</p>\n          <p>{rider.last_login_at ? new Date(rider.last_login_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN') : '—'}</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"

> AGENT

Done. All 9 tasks completed and the production build passes cleanly with 16 new routes.

## What was built

**Database** — `scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql`
- `delivery_boys` table (riders) + `rider-id-proofs` private bucket
- Orders gain `delivery_type`, `delivery_status`, address, `handover_otp`, and stage timestamps

**Rider side** — `/rider`
- Signup with ID-photo upload → owner approval → 8-char activation code → phone+password login
- Dashboard with `Available` and `My deliveries` tabs, auto-refresh every 15s
- State machine: accept → picked-up → out-for-delivery → deliver (rider types OTP from customer)
- Optimistic-lock claim so two riders can't both win the same order

**Consumer side**
- New radio at checkout: `Pickup myself` vs `Home delivery` (no fee — owner collects manually)
- Address / landmark / pincode / alt-phone form
- Server generates one 4-digit `handover_otp` per checkout batch
- Order detail page: status timeline, the OTP shown only after rider is assigned, tap-to-call rider button

**Farmer side**
- Each order card shows a `🛵 Home delivery` tag and the rider's name + tap-to-call once assigned

**Owner panel** — `/admin` (password-gated via `ADMIN_PASSWORD` env var)
- Riders tab: approve, suspend, reinstate, view ID-proof photo, re-issue activation code
- Deliveries tab: all home-delivery orders with handover OTP, assigned rider, and a dropdown to reassign or un-assign
- 20s auto-refresh

**Security**
- Separate HMAC-signed cookies per role (`yff_consumer`, `yff_rider`, `yff_admin`), all namespaced so a token from one role can't be replayed against another's endpoints
- scrypt password hashing, rate-limited login + OTP + activation, constant-time OTP comparison
- Service-role-only DB access; consumer sees rider's name+phone only, never ID proof or password
- Handover OTP never sent to rider — they must type what the customer reads aloud

## Two things you need to do before this works

1. Run `scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql` in the Supabase SQL editor
2. Add `ADMIN_PASSWORD=<your-8-plus-char-password>` to your `.env.local` and Vercel env (uses your existing `SESSION_SECRET`)

This is the password you'll use to log in at `/admin/login`. It's a 20-character random value generated with `openssl rand`. You can change it later by editing `.env.local` locally and updating the Vercel env var — keep them the same so logins work in both dev and prod.

After you add it in Vercel:
1. Trigger a redeploy so the new env var loads
2. Visit `/admin/login` on the live site and use the password above
3. Run `scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql` in Supabase if you haven't yet — without it, the rider signup and admin pages will return a "table is missing" error

how delivery boy will login

> AGENT

Here's the full delivery boy login flow we built — three steps the first time, one step every day after that.

## First time only (3 one-time steps)

### Step 1 — Rider signs himself up
He opens `https://yourdomain.com/rider/signup` on his phone and fills:

- Full name
- Phone number (primary)
- Alternate phone (optional)
- Password (he sets it — at least 6 characters)
- Vehicle type (bike / scooter / cycle / auto / other)
- Vehicle number
- Areas he can deliver to
- Photo of his ID proof (Aadhaar / DL)

He submits → sees **"Application received. Owner will call you with an activation code."**

At this point his account exists but it is **inactive** — he cannot log in yet.

### Step 2 — You (owner) approve him
You log in to `https://yourdomain.com/admin/login` with the admin password.

In the **Riders** tab, his application appears with the ID-proof photo. You tap **"Approve & generate code"**.

The system creates a code like `YFF-K9PRTM47` and shows it on your screen. You tell this code to the rider in person or over a phone call.

### Step 3 — Rider activates his account
He opens `https://yourdomain.com/rider/activate`, enters his phone number and the code you gave him, and taps **Activate**.

Account is now active.

---

## Every day after that — just login

He opens `https://yourdomain.com/rider/login`, enters:

- His phone number
- His password (the one he set during signup)

Done — he lands on `https://yourdomain.com/rider/dashboard` and sees:

- **Available** tab — orders waiting for a delivery boy
- **My deliveries** tab — orders he has already accepted

He stays logged in for 30 days (cookie lasts that long), so he typically only re-logs in if he clears his browser.

---

## Quick summary diagram

```
NEW RIDER
  ↓
/rider/signup  (fills form, uploads ID)
  ↓
Status: pending_approval  ←─  cannot log in yet
  ↓
OWNER opens /admin → Riders tab → "Approve & generate code"
  ↓
Status: approved  ←─  code issued, still cannot log in
  ↓
Owner shares code with rider (in person / call)
  ↓
RIDER opens /rider/activate, enters phone + code
  ↓
Status: active  ←─  now he can log in
  ↓
RIDER opens /rider/login, enters phone + password
  ↓
/rider/dashboard
```

---

## Why three steps the first time?

It's deliberately two-factor. A random person can't become a delivery boy just by filling the signup form — your approval is required. And approval alone isn't enough either — the rider needs the code you personally gave him to actually activate. This protects you from fake riders signing up just to see customer phone numbers.

## If the rider is stuck

- **"Your application is waiting for owner approval"** → check your `/admin` panel, approve him
- **"Your account is approved. Activate it with the code first"** → he hasn't entered the code at `/rider/activate` yet
- **"Wrong activation code"** → tell him the code again (case-insensitive, but characters like `YFF-...` matter)
- **"Wrong password"** → he forgot his password. For now, there's no self-serve reset — you can delete his row from the `delivery_boys` table in Supabase so he can re-sign up, or we can add a password reset flow later
- **"No rider account for this number"** → he never completed signup. Send him to `/rider/signup`

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

const ROLE_KEY = 'yff_role'

export default function RoleGateModal() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)

  // Mount: open the modal if the visitor hasn't picked a role yet. Done in a
  // useEffect so SSR doesn't render anything (avoids hydration mismatch).
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const existing = localStorage.getItem(ROLE_KEY)
      if (!existing) setOpen(true)
    } catch {
      // localStorage might be blocked in private modes — just skip the gate.
    }
  }, [])

  if (!open) return null

  const choose = (role: 'consumer' | 'farmer') => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(ROLE_KEY, role) } catch { /* ignore */ }
    setOpen(false)
    if (role === 'farmer') router.push('/farmer/dashboard')
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[150] bg-black/60 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center px-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-5 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="text-center space-y-1">
          <p className="text-3xl">🌿</p>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">
            Welcome to YourFamilyFarmer
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
            యువర్ ఫ్యామిలీ ఫార్మర్‌కి స్వాగతం
          </p>
        </div>

        <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 text-center leading-snug">
          Tell us how you&apos;d like to start.<br />
          మీరు ఎలా ప్రారంభించాలనుకుంటున్నారు?
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => choose('consumer')}
            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-extrabold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base active:bg-green-800 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 leading-tight"
          >
            🛒 I&apos;m a Buyer<br />
            <span className="font-semibold text-sm opacity-90">నేను కొనుగోలుదారుని</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => choose('farmer')}
            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-extrabold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base active:bg-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 leading-tight"
          >
            🧑‍🌾 I&apos;m a Farmer<br />
            <span className="font-semibold text-sm opacity-90">నేను రైతుని</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 text-center">
          You can switch roles anytime from the top menu.<br />
          ఎప్పుడైనా పైన ఉన్న మెనూ నుండి మార్చవచ్చు.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```
